           This file contains birth records from the Parish
           of Rohrbach, South Russia for the decade 186x.
           This information was compiled by Dale Wahl and 
           coworkers March 24, 1995 from the St. Petersburg
           Lutheran Evangelical archives published by the LDS. 
           -re (9-17-1995)
